Title: A Discourse of the Pastoral Care
Creator: Gilbert Burnet, Lord Bishop of Sarum (Salisbury)
Publisher: London: printed by R. R. for Ric. Chiswell, at the Rose and Crown in St Paul's Church-yard
Publication Date: 1692
Rights: Public Domain (published 1692, more than 95 years ago; public domain by date under the project's rolling rule; the item carries no rights field, so the date rule alone applies)
Source: Internet Archive item dispasto00burn (https://archive.org/details/dispasto00burn), the item's own dispasto00burn_djvu.txt full-text OCR derivative; contributing library per the item metadata: Princeton Theological Seminary Library; host metadata date: 1692. Fetched directly (curl/urllib, raw bytes) by the Library thread (Era VIII pass), 2026-10-23. sha256 of the raw fetched bytes: c2e7e5ff1c62aba05d22d7a7fca1844e525099ac7fdd807ad9aa6f5cec7d96c5.
Provenance note: Header prepended by the Library thread; nothing else altered, no OCR error corrected, nothing retyped. Princeton Theological Seminary Library scan of the first edition; good OCR with the long s kept.
Content note: Burnet's manual for the clergy after the Revolution of 1688, dedicated to Queen Mary: the dignity of sacred employments, the Scripture rules for those who minister and the corruptions they condemn, the sense of the primitive church and its canons, the Church of England's own rules, the preparation of ordinands, the functions and labours of the clergy, and preaching.
